Current source of the opcode bodies:\n\n```cpp\n[[gnu::always_inline]]\ninline void op_filter_formant(ExecutionContext& ctx, const Instruction& inst) {\n    float* out_l = 1.0)\n    const auto* ext = ctx.states->get_if<ExtendedParams<2>>(ext_params_state_id(inst.state_id));\n    const float* dry_buf = nullptr; float dry_const = 0.0f;\n    const float* wet_buf = nullptr; float wet_const = 1.0f;\n    if (ext) {\n        const auto& d = ext->params[0];\n        if (d.is_constant()) dry_const = d.constant; else dry_buf = ctx.buffers->get(d.buffer_idx);\n        const auto& w = ext->params[1];\n        if (w.is_constant()) wet_const = w.constant; else wet_buf = ctx.buffers->get(w.buffer_idx);\n    }\n\n    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < BLOCK_SIZE; ++i) {\n        float va = std::clamp(vowel_a[i], 0.0f, 4.0f);\n        float vb = std::clamp(vowel_b[i], 0.0f, 4.0f);\n        float m = std::clamp(morph[i], 0.0f, 1.0f);\n        float q = std::clamp(q_in[i], 1.0f, 20.0f);\n        float dry = drywet::coeff(dry_buf, i, dry_const);\n        float wet = drywet::coeff(wet_buf, i, wet_const);\n\n        // Check if we need to recalculate formant targets\n        if (va != state.last_vowel_a || vb != state.last_vowel_b ||\n            m != state.last_morph || q != state.last_q) {\n\n            state.last_vowel_a = va;\n            state.last_vowel_b = vb;\n            state.last_morph = m;\n            state.last_q = q;\n\n            // Interpolate between vowel indices (with fractional support)\n            int idx_a = static_cast<int>(va);\n            int idx_b = static_cast<int>(vb);\n            float frac_a = va - static_cast<float>(idx_a);\n            float frac_b = vb - static_cast<float>(idx_b);\n\n            idx_a = std::min(idx_a, 4);\n            idx_b = std::min(idx_b, 4);\n            int idx_a2 = std::min(idx_a + 1, 4);\n            int idx_b2 = std::min(idx_b + 1, 4);\n\n            // Get formants for vowel A (with fractional interpolation)\n            float f1_a = VOWEL_TABLE[idx_a].f1 * (1.0f - frac_a) + VOWEL_TABLE[idx_a2].f1 * frac_a;\n            float f2_a = VOWEL_TABLE[idx_a].f2 * (1.0f - frac_a) + VOWEL_TABLE[idx_a2].f2 * frac_a;\n            float f3_a = VOWEL_TABLE[idx_a].f3 * (1.0f - frac_a) + VOWEL_TABLE[idx_a2].f3 * frac_a;\n            float g1_a = VOWEL_TABLE[idx_a].g1 * (1.0f - frac_a) + VOWEL_TABLE[idx_a2].g1 * frac_a;\n            float g2_a = VOWEL_TABLE[idx_a].g2 * (1.0f - frac_a) + VOWEL_TABLE[idx_a2].g2 * frac_a;\n            float g3_a = VOWEL_TABLE[idx_a].g3 * (1.0f - frac_a) + VOWEL_TABLE[idx_a2].g3 * frac_a;\n\n            // Get formants for vowel B\n            float f1_b = VOWEL_TABLE[idx_b].f1 * (1.0f - frac_b) + VOWEL_TABLE[idx_b2].f1 * frac_b;\n            float f2_b = VOWEL_TABLE[idx_b].f2 * (1.0f - frac_b) + VOWEL_TABLE[idx_b2].f2 * frac_b;\n            float f3_b = VOWEL_TABLE[idx_b].f3 * (1.0f - frac_b) + VOWEL_TABLE[idx_b2].f3 * frac_b;\n   float g3_b = VOWEL_TABLE[idx_b].g3 * (1.0f - frac_b) + VOWEL_TABLE[idx_b2].g3 * frac_b;\n\n            // Morph between A and B\n            state.f1 = f1_a * (1.0f - m) + f1_b * m;\n            state.f2 = f2_a * (1.0f - m) + f2_b * m;\n            state.f3 = f3_a * (1.0f - m) + f3_b * m;\n            state.g1 = g1_a * (1.0f - m) + g1_b * m;\n            state.g2 = g2_a * (1.0f - m) + g2_b * m;\n            state.g3 = g3_a * (1.0f - m) + g3_b * m;\n        }\n\n        // Chamberlin SVF coefficients with bilinear pre-warping (shared across channels)\n        auto prewarp_coef = [&](float freq) -> float {\n            float w = PI * std::min(freq, ctx.sample_rate * 0.48f) / ctx.sample_rate;\n            return std::min(1.9f, 2.0f * std::tan(w));\n        };\n        float f1_coef = prewarp_coef(state.f1);\n        float f2_coef = prewarp_coef(state.f2);\n        float f3_coef = prewarp_coef(state.f3);\n        float q_coef = 1.0f / q;\n\n        for (std::size_t ch = 0; ch < 2; ++ch) {\n            float x = (ch == 0) ? input[i] : (stereo_in ? input_r[i] : input[i]);\n\n            // Bandpass 1 (F1 - first formant)\n            float hp1 = x - state.bp1_z1[ch] * q_coef - state.bp1_z2[ch];\n            float bp1 = state.bp1_z1[ch] + f1_coef * hp1;\n            float lp1 = state.bp1_z2[ch] + f1_coef * state.bp1_z1[ch];\n            state.bp1_z1[ch] = clamp_audio(bp1);\n            state.bp1_z2[ch] = clamp_audio(lp1);\n\n            // Bandpass 2 (F2 - second formant)\n            float hp2 = x - state.bp2_z1[ch] * q_coef - state.bp2_z2[ch];\n            float bp2 = state.bp2_z1[ch] + f2_coef * hp2;\n            float lp2 = state.bp2_z2[ch] + f2_coef * state.bp2_z1[ch];\n            state.bp2_z1[ch] = clamp_audio(bp2);\n            state.bp2_z2[ch] = clamp_audio(lp2);\n\n            // Bandpass 3 (F3 - third formant)\n            float hp3 = x - state.bp3_z1[ch] * q_coef - state.bp3_z2[ch];\n            float bp3 = state.bp3_z1[ch] + f3_coef * hp3;\n            float lp3 = state.bp3_z2[ch] + f3_coef * state.bp3_z1[ch];\n            state.bp3_z1[ch] = clamp_audio(bp3);\n            state.bp3_z2[ch] = clamp_audio(lp3);\n\n            // Sum bandpasses with formant gains\n            float processed = bp1 * state.g1 + bp2 * state.g2 + bp3 * state.g3;\n            (ch == 0 ? out_l : out_r)[i] = drywet::mix(x, processed, dry, wet);\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\nCost on the gate machine (median ns per opcode-block, lower is better):\n\n| Opcode | Original scalar | Current (accepted so far) | Speedup so far |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| Keep\nthe scalar body and add a kernel in front of it:\n\n```cpp\n#include \"../dsp/simd.hpp\"\n\nnamespace kernels {\n#if CEDAR_SIMD_X86\nCEDAR_TARGET_AVX2 inline void mul_avx2(const float* a, const float* b, float* out) noexcept {\n    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < BLOCK_SIZE; i += 8)\n        _mm256_store_ps(out + i, _mm256_mul_ps(_mm256_load_ps(a + i), _mm256_load_ps(b + i)));\n}\n#endif\n}\n\ninline void op_mul(ExecutionContext& ctx, const Instruction& inst) {\n    // ... resolve a, b, out ...\n#if CEDAR_SIMD_X86\n    if (simd::has(simd::Isa::Avx2)) { kernels::mul_avx2(a, b, out); return; }\n#endif\n    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < BLOCK_SIZE; ++i) out[i] = a[i] * b[i];  // fallback stays\n}\n```\n\n- `CEDAR_TARGET_AVX2` is `[[gnu::target(\"avx2\")]]`; no compiler flags change.\n  FMA is **not** enabled (it would change rounding).\n- Buffers from `ctx.buffers->get()` are 32-byte aligned; `BLOCK_SIZE` is 128.\n- You may also add NEON (`CEDAR_SIMD_NEON`) or WASM SIMD128\n  (`CEDAR_SIMD_WASM`) paths; they are measured later on other machines, not\n  gated here.\n\n## Rules (enforced by the verifier \u2014 violations are rejected automatically)\n\n1. ============================================================================\n   148\t// Moog-Style Ladder Filter\n   149\t// ============================================================================\n   150\t\n   151\t// Soft saturation function (fast tanh approximation)\n   152\t// Provides analog-like nonlinearity in the feedback path\n   153\t[[gnu::always_inline]]\n   154\tinline float soft_clip(float x) {\n   155\t    // Pade approximant of tanh, accurate for |x| < 3\n   156\t    if (x > 3.0f) return 1.0f;\n   157\t    if (x < -3.0f) return -1.0f;\n   158\t    float x2 = x * x;\n   159\t    return x * (27.0f + x2) / (27.0f + 9.0f * x2);\n   160\t}\n   161\t\n   162\t// Default constants for Moog ladder filter\n   163\tconstexpr float MOOG_MAX_RESONANCE_DEFAULT = 4.0f;\n   164\tconstexpr float MOOG_INPUT_SCALE_DEFAULT = 0.5f;\n   165\t\n   166\t// FILTER_MOOG: 4-pole (24dB/oct) Moog-style ladder filter\n   167\t// in0: input signal\n   168\t// in1: cutoff frequency (Hz)\n   169\t// in2: resonance (0.0-max_resonance, self-oscillates at ~4.0)\n   170\t// in3: max_resonance - self-oscillation threshold (default 4.0)\n   171\t// in4: input_scale - preamp drive/saturation (default 0.5)\n   172\t//\n   173\t// Based on the Huovilainen improved model for digital Moog filters\n   174\t// Features nonlinear saturation in the feedback path for analog character\n   175\t[[gnu::always_inline]]\n   176\tinline void op_filter_moog(ExecutionContext& ctx, const Instruction& inst) {\n   177\t    float* out_l = ctx.buffers->get(inst.out_buffer);\n   178\t    float* out_r = ctx.buffers->get(static_cast<std::uint16_t>(inst.out_buffer + 1));\n   179\t    const float* input = ctx.buffers->get(inst.inputs[0]);\n   180\t    const bool stereo_in = (inst.flags & InstructionFlag::STEREO_INPUT) != 0;\n   181\t    const float* input_r = stereo_in\n   182\t        ? ctx.buffers->get(static_cast<std::uint16_t>(inst.inputs[0] + 1))\n   183\t        : nullptr;\n   184\t    const float* freq = ctx.buffers->get(inst.inputs[1]);\n   185\t    const float* res = ctx.buffers->get(inst.inputs[2]);\n   186\t    const float* max_res_in = ctx.buffers->get(inst.inputs[3]);\n   187\t    const float* input_scale_in = ctx.buffers->get(inst.inputs[4]);\n   188\t    auto& state = ctx.states->get_or_create<MoogState>(inst.state_id);\n   189\t\n   190\t    // ExtendedParams<2>: ext[0] = dry (default 0.0), ext[1] = wet (default 1.0)\n   191\t    const auto* ext = ctx.states->get_if<ExtendedParams<2>>(ext_params_state_id(inst.state_id));\n   192\t    const float* dry_buf = nullptr; float dry_const = 0.0f;\n   193\t    const float* wet_buf = nullptr; float wet_const = 1.0f;\n   194\t    if (ext) {\n   195\t        const auto& d = ext->params[0];\n   196\t        if (d.is_constant()) dry_const = d.constant; else dry_buf = ctx.buffers->get(d.buffer_idx);\n   197\t        const auto& w = ext->params[1];\n   198\t        if (w.is_constant()) wet_const = w.constant; else wet_buf = ctx.buffers->get(w.buffer_idx);\n   199\t    }\n   200\t\n   201\t    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < BLOCK_SIZE; ++i) {\n   202\t        float cutoff = freq[i];\n   203\t        float resonance = res[i];\n   204\t\n   205\t        // Runtime tunable parameters (use defaults if zero/negative)\n   206\t        float max_resonance = max_res_in[i] > 0.0f ? max_res_in[i] : MOOG_MAX_RESONANCE_DEFAULT;\n   207\t        float input_scale = input_scale_in[i] > 0.0f ? input_scale_in[i] : MOOG_INPUT_SCALE_DEFAULT;\n   208\t        float dry = drywet::coeff(dry_buf, i, dry_const);\n   209\t        float wet = drywet::coeff(wet_buf, i, wet_const);\n   210\t\n   211\t        // Update coefficients if parameters changed (shared across channels)\n   212\t        if (cutoff != state.last_freq || resonance != state.last_res) {\n   213\t            state.last_freq = cutoff;\n   214\t            state.last_res = resonance;\n   215\t\n   216\t            // Frequency warping for digital implementation\n   217\t            // Clamp cutoff to prevent instability at very high frequencies\n   218\t            float f = std::clamp(cutoff / ctx.sample_rate, 0.0f, 0.45f);\n   219\t\n   220\t            // Compute g coefficient using tan for frequency warping\n   221\t            state.g = std::tan(PI * f);\n   222\t\n   223\t            // Resonance coefficient (0 to max_resonance range, self-oscillates near 4)\n   224\t            state.k = std::clamp(resonance, 0.0f, max_resonance);\n   225\t        }\n   226\t\n   227\t        // G = g / (1 + g) for each stage (shared)\n   228\t        float G = state.g / (1.0f + state.g);\n   229\t\n   230\t        for (std::size_t ch = 0; ch < 2; ++ch) {\n   231\t            float xin = (ch == 0) ? input[i] : (stereo_in ? input_r[i] : input[i]);\n   232\t\n   233\t            // Feedback from last stage output with nonlinear saturation\n   234\t            float feedback = state.k * soft_clip(state.stage[ch][3]);\n   235\t\n   236\t            // Input with feedback subtracted (negative feedback loop)\n   237\t            float x = xin - feedback;\n   238\t\n   239\t            // Soft clip the input to prevent harsh clipping at high input levels\n   240\t            x = soft_clip(x * input_scale) * (1.0f / input_scale);\n   241\t\n   242\t            // 4 cascaded 1-pole lowpass stages using trapezoidal integration\n   243\t            for (int j = 0; j < 4; ++j) {\n   244\t                float input_stage = (j == 0) ? x : state.stage[ch][j - 1];\n   245\t\n   246\t                // Trapezoidal integration (implicit Euler)\n   247\t                float v = G * (input_stage - state.delay[ch][j]);\n   248\t                float y = v + state.delay[ch][j];\n   249\t\n   250\t                // Update delay and stage output\n   251\t                state.delay[ch][j] = y + v;\n   252\t                state.stage[ch][j] = y;\n   253\t\n   254\t                // Apply soft saturation between stages for analog character\n   255\t                if (j < 3) {\n   256\t                    state.stage[ch][j] = soft_clip(state.stage[ch][j]);\n   257\t                }\n   258\t            }\n   259\t\n   260\t            // Output is the 4-pole lowpass\n   261\t            float processed = clamp_audio(state.stage[ch][3]);\n   262\t            (ch == 0 ? out_l : out_r)[i] = drywet::mix(xin, processed, dry, wet);\n   263\t        }\n   264\t    }\n   265\t}\n   266\t\n   267\t// ============================================================================\n   268\t// Diode Ladder Filter (TB-303 Acid)\n   269\t// ============================================================================\n   270\t\n   271\t// Diode nonlinearity: hyperbolic sine approximation\n   272\t// True diode: I = I_s * (exp(V / V_t) - 1), but sinh provides symmetric behavior\n   273\t[[gnu::always_inline]]\n   274\tinline float diode_sinh(float x) {\n   275\t    // Fast sinh approximation for |x| < 4\n   276\t    // sinh(x) = x + x^3/6 + x^5/120 for small x\n   277\t    // For larger values, use (exp(x) - exp(-x)) / 2\n   278\t    if (x > 4.0f) return 27.29f + 0.5f * std::exp(x);  // exp(4)/2 \u2248 27.29\n   279\t    if (x < -4.0f) return -27.29f - 0.5f * std::exp(-x);\n   280\t\n   281\t    float x2 = x * x;\n   282\t    return x * (1.0f + x2 * (0.166667f + x2 * 0.00833333f));\n   283\t}\n   284\t\n   285\t// Derivative of sinh for Newton-Raphson\n   286\t[[gnu::always_inline]]\n   287\tinline float diode_cosh(float x) {\n   288\t    // cosh(x) = 1 + x^2/2 + x^4/24\n   289\t    if (std::abs(x) > 4.0f) return 0.5f * (std::exp(std::abs(x)));\n   290\t\n   291\t    float x2 = x * x;\n   292\t    return 1.0f + x2 * (0.5f + x2 * 0.0416667f);\n   293\t}\n   294\t\n   295\t// Default constants for diode ladder filter (used when inputs provide 0 or negative values)\n   296\t// VT: Thermal voltage - affects diode curve sharpness (real diode ~0.026V)\n   297\t// FB_GAIN: Feedback multiplier to compensate for VT attenuation in feedback path\n   298\tconstexpr float DIODE_VT_DEFAULT = 0.026f;\n   299\tconstexpr float DIODE_FB_GAIN_DEFAULT = 10.0f;\n   300\t\n   301\t// FILTER_DIODE: ZDF 4-pole diode ladder filter\n   302\t// in0: input signal\n   303\t// in1: cutoff frequency (Hz)\n   304\t// in2: resonance (0.0-4.0, self-oscillates at ~3.5+)\n   305\t// in3: vt - thermal voltage (affects diode curve sharpness, default 0.026)\n   306\t// in4: fb_gain - feedback gain multiplier (compensates VT attenuation, default 10.0)\n   307\t//\n   308\t// Based on the Roland TB-303 filter topology with diode nonlinearity.\n   309\t// Uses Newton-Raphson iteration for implicit integration.\n   310\t// The diode nonlinearity creates the characteristic \"squelchy\" acid sound.\n   311\t[[gnu::always_inline]]\n   312\tinline void op_filter_diode(ExecutionContext& ctx, const Instruction& inst) {\n   313\t    float* out_l = ctx.buffers->get(inst.out_buffer);\n   314\t    float* 1.0f, 0.6f, 0.3f},     // U (as in \"boot\")\n   425\t    {400.0f, 2000.0f, 2550.0f, 1.0f, 0.45f, 0.25f},  // E (as in \"bed\")\n   426\t    {400.0f, 800.0f, 2600.0f, 1.0f, 0.5f, 0.2f}      // O (as in \"bought\")\n   427\t};\n   428\t\n   429\t// FILTER_FORMANT: 3-band parallel vowel morphing filter\n   430\t// in0: input signal\n   431\t// in1: vowel_a (0-4, selects first vowel: A/I/U/E/O)\n   432\t// in2: vowel_b (0-4, selects second vowel)\n   433\t// in3: morph (0-1, interpolates between vowel_a and vowel_b)\n   434\t// in4: q (resonance/bandwidth, 1-20)\n   435\t//\n   436\t// Creates vocal-like filter sweeps by morphing between vowel formants.\n   437\t// Uses 3 parallel Chamberlin SVF bandpass filters.\n   438\t[[gnu::always_inline]]\n   439\tinline void op_filter_formant(ExecutionContext& ctx, const Instruction& inst) {\n   440\t    float* out_l = ctx.buffers->get(inst.out_buffer);\n   441\t    float* out_r = ctx.buffers->get(static_cast<std::uint16_t>(inst.out_buffer + 1));\n   442\t    const float* input = ctx.buffers->get(inst.inputs[0]);\n   443\t    const bool stereo_in = (inst.flags & InstructionFlag::STEREO_INPUT) != 0;\n   444\t    const float* input_r = stereo_in\n   445\t        ? ctx.buffers->get(static_cast<std::uint16_t>(inst.inputs[0] + 1))\n   446\t        : nullptr;\n   447\t    const float* vowel_a = ctx.buffers->get(inst.inputs[1]);\n   448\t    const float* vowel_b = ctx.buffers->get(inst.inputs[2]);\n   449\t    const float* morph = ctx.buffers->get(inst.inputs[3]);\n   450\t    const float* q_in = ctx.buffers->get(inst.inputs[4]);\n   451\t    auto& state = ctx.states->get_or_create<FormantState>(inst.state_id);\n   452\t\n   453\t    // ExtendedParams<2>: ext[0] = dry (default 0.0), ext[1] = wet (default 1.0)\n   454\t    const auto* ext = ctx.states->get_if<ExtendedParams<2>>(ext_params_state_id(inst.state_id));\n   455\t    const float* dry_buf = nullptr; float dry_const = 0.0f;\n   456\t    const float* wet_buf = nullptr; float wet_const = 1.0f;\n   457\t    if (ext) {\n   458\t        const auto& d = ext->params[0];\n   459\t        if (d.is_constant()) dry_const = d.constant; else dry_buf = ctx.buffers->get(d.buffer_idx);\n   460\t        const auto& w = ext->params[1];\n   461\t        if (w.is_constant()) wet_const = w.constant; else wet_buf = ctx.buffers->get(w.buffer_idx);\n   462\t    }\n   463\t\n   464\t    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < BLOCK_SIZE; ++i) {\n   465\t        float va = std::clamp(vowel_a[i], 0.0f, 4.0f);\n   466\t        float vb = std::clamp(vowel_b[i], 0.0f, 4.0f);\n   467\t        float m = std::clamp(morph[i], 0.0f, 1.0f);\n   468\t        float q = std::clamp(q_in[i], 1.0f, 20.0f);\n   469\t        float dry = drywet::coeff(dry_buf, i, dry_const);\n   470\t        float wet = drywet::coeff(wet_buf, i, wet_const);\n   471\t\n   472\t        // Check if we need to recalculate formant targets\n   473\t        if (va != state.last_vowel_a || vb != state.last_vowel_b ||\n   474\t            m != state.last_morph || q != state.last_q) {\n   475\t\n   476\t            state.last_vowel_a = va;\n   477\t            state.last_vowel_b = vb;\n   478\t            state.last_morph = m;\n   479\t            state.last_q = q;\n   480\t\n   481\t            // Interpolate between vowel indices (with fractional support)\n   482\t            int idx_a = static_cast<int>(va);\n   483\t            int idx_b = static_cast<int>(vb);\n   484\t            float frac_a = va - static_cast<float>(idx_a);\n   485\t            float frac_b = vb - static_cast<float>(idx_b);\n   486\t\n   487\t            idx_a = std::min(idx_a, 4);\n   488\t            idx_b = std::min(idx_b, 4);\n   489\t            int idx_a2 = std::min(idx_a + 1, 4);\n   490\t            int idx_b2 = std::min(idx_b + 1, 4);\n   491\t\n   492\t            // Get formants for vowel A (with fractional interpolation)\n   493\t            float f1_a = VOWEL_TABLE[idx_a].f1 * (1.0f - frac_a) + VOWEL_TABLE[idx_a2].f1 * frac_a;\n   494\t            float f2_a = VOWEL_TABLE[idx_a].f2 * (1.0f - frac_a) + VOWEL_TABLE[idx_a2].f2 * frac_a;\n   495\t            float f3_a = VOWEL_TABLE[idx_a].f3 * (1.0f - frac_a) + VOWEL_TABLE[idx_a2].f3 * frac_a;\n   496\t            float g1_a = VOWEL_TABLE[idx_a].g1 * (1.0f - frac_a) + VOWEL_TABLE[idx_a2].g1 * frac_a;\n   497\t            float g2_a = VOWEL_TABLE[idx_a].g2 * (1.0f - frac_a) + VOWEL_TABLE[idx_a2].g2 * frac_a;\n   498\t            float g3_a = VOWEL_TABLE[idx_a].g3 * (1.0f - frac_a) + VOWEL_TABLE[idx_a2].g3 * frac_a;\n   499\t\n   500\t            // Get formants for vowel B\n   501\t            float f1_b = VOWEL_TABLE[idx_b].f1 * (1.0f - frac_b) + VOWEL_TABLE[idx_b2].f1 * frac_b;\n   502\t            float f2_b = VOWEL_TABLE[idx_b].f2 * (1.0f - frac_b) + VOWEL_TABLE[idx_b2].f2 * frac_b;\n   503\t            float f3_b = VOWEL_TABLE[idx_b].f3 * (1.0f - frac_b) + VOWEL_TABLE[idx_b2].f3 * frac_b;\n   504\t            float g1_b = VOWEL_TABLE[idx_b].g1 * (1.0f - frac_b) + VOWEL_TABLE[idx_b2].g1 * frac_b;\n   505\t            float g2_b = VOWEL_TABLE[idx_b].g2 * (1.0f - frac_b) + VOWEL_TABLE[idx_b2].g2 * frac_b;\n   506\t            float g3_b = VOWEL_TABLE[idx_b].g3 * (1.0f - frac_b) + VOWEL_TABLE[idx_b2].g3 * frac_b;\n   507\t\n   508\t            // Morph between A and B\n   509\t            state.f1 = f1_a * (1.0f - m) + f1_b * m;\n   510\t            state.f2 = f2_a * (1.0f - m) + f2_b * m;\n   511\t            state.f3 = f3_a * (1.0f - m) + f3_b * m;\n   512\t            state.g1 = g1_a * (1.0f - m) + g1_b * m;\n   513\t            state.g2 = g2_a * (1.0f - m) + g2_b * m;\n   514\t            state.g3 = g3_a * (1.0f - m) + g3_b * m;\n   515\t        }\n   516\t\n   517\t        // Chamberlin SVF coefficients with bilinear pre-warping (shared across channels)\n   518\t        auto prewarp_coef = [&](float freq) -> float {\n   519\t            float w = PI * std::min(freq, ctx.sample_rate * 0.48f) / ctx.sample_rate;\n   520\t            return std::min(1.9f, 2.0f * std::tan(w));\n   521\t        };\n   522\t        float f1_coef = prewarp_coef(state.f1);\n   523\t        float f2_coef = prewarp_coef(state.f2);\n   524\t        float f3_coef = prewarp_coef(state.f3);\n   525\t        float q_coef = 1.0f / q;\n   526\t\n   527\t        for (std::size_t ch = 0; ch < 2; ++ch) {\n   528\t            float x = (ch == 0) ? input[i] : (stereo_in ? input_r[i] : input[i]);\n   529\t\n   530\t            // Bandpass 1 (F1 - first formant)\n   531\t            float hp1 = x - state.bp1_z1[ch] * q_coef - state.bp1_z2[ch];\n   532\t            float bp1 = state.bp1_z1[ch] + f1_coef * hp1;\n   533\t            float lp1 = state.bp1_z2[ch] + f1_coef * state.bp1_z1[ch];\n   534\t            state.bp1_z1[ch] = clamp_audio(bp1);\n   535\t            state.bp1_z2[ch] = clamp_audio(lp1);\n   536\t\n   537\t            // Bandpass 2 (F2 - second formant)\n   538\t            float hp2 = x - state.bp2_z1[ch] * q_coef - state.bp2_z2[ch];\n   539\t            float bp2 = state.bp2_z1[ch] + f2_coef * hp2;\n   540\t            float lp2 = state.bp2_z2[ch] + f2_coef * state.bp2_z1[ch];\n   541\t            state.bp2_z1[ch] = clamp_audio(bp2);\n   542\t            state.bp2_z2[ch] = clamp_audio(lp2);\n   543\t\n   544\t            // Bandpass 3 (F3 - third formant)\n   545\t            float hp3 = x - state.bp3_z1[ch] * q_coef - state.bp3_z2[ch];\n   546\t            float bp3 = state.bp3_z1[ch] + f3_coef * hp3;\n   547\t            float lp3 = state.bp3_z2[ch] + f3_coef * state.bp3_z1[ch];\n   548\t            state.bp3_z1[ch] = clamp_audio(bp3);\n   549\t            state.bp3_z2[ch] = clamp_audio(lp3);\n   550\t\n   551\t            // Sum bandpasses with formant gains\n   552\t            float processed = bp1 * state.g1 + bp2 * state.g2 + bp3 * state.g3;\n   553\t            (ch == 0 ? out_l : out_r)[i] = drywet::mix(x, processed, dry, wet);\n   554\t        }\n   555\t    }\n   556\t}\n   557\t\n   558\t// ============================================================================\n   559\t// Sallen-Key Filter (MS-20 Style)"

**L2 (Phase 2) \u2014 SHIPPED.** Shipped first: the `#inline` annotation (lexer `#` token + statement-position parsing) and recursion rejection (E240/E244/E246/E249). Shipped completing L2: eligible non-`#inline` user `fn`s called from \u22652 sites compile **once** into a subprogram block and dispatch at runtime via a real `BLOCK_CALL` opcode \u2014 body stored once in `ProgramSlot::instructions[]`, relieving program-size / i-cache pressure. **Design pivot from \u00a74.2 \u2014 the swap-time `expand_block_calls()` model was abandoned.** Compile-time expansion produces audio bytecode byte-identical to per-site inlining (zero size/i-cache benefit \u2014 see \u00a76.1); instead L2 reuses L3's already-shipped runtime subprogram table (`ProgramSlot::blocks[]`, the block-aware load handshake, `set_state_id_xor` isolation). `BLOCK_CALL` carries `block_id` in `inst.rate`, caller args in `inst.inputs[0..4]`, result in `inst.out_buffer`, a per-call-site disambiguator in `inst.state_id`; `BlockEntry` gained `param_base` + `body_output` (12 bytes). `RET` stays a reserved no-op \u2014 body length lives in `BlockEntry::length`. Eligibility = speculative-compile-with-rollback (cheap signature gate, then a one-shot body compile rolled back on any error / empty-or-oversize body / nested dispatch opcode); ineligible or non-dispatchable call sites (string/array/record/`_` args) fall back to inlining \u2014 a pure optimization. Per-call-site state isolation + hot-swap continuity via a `block:<fn>@callsite_<N>` path hash XOR-composed by the VM. Rollback knob: the `inline_all_fns` compiler option forces the pre-L2 inline-everywhere behavior. **`BLOCK_BIND` (\u00a74.2) shipped as a follow-up (2026-05-20):** the >5-param slot path is now built \u2014 `fn`s with 6..`MAX_SHARED_FN_PARAMS` (32) parameters are shareable. Params 0..4 ride `BLOCK_CALL.inputs[0..4]`; params 5..N-1 ride a contiguous run of `BLOCK_BIND` opcodes (slot index in `rate`, source buffer in `inputs[0]`) emitted immediately before the `BLOCK_CALL`. The VM dispatch loop scans the run and copies the extra slots into the body's convention param bank. `fn`s beyond 32 params still fall back to inlining (graceful, never an error). **Dispatch-uniformity followup (2026-05-24, `d95aa8b`):** the L2 ship wired `BLOCK_CALL` / `BLOCK_BIND` / `SKIP_IF_*` / `LOOP_STATIC` into `execute_program`'s main IP loop but left every subprogram-body runner (`run_voice_pool`, `run_foreach_per_iteration`, `run_foreach_shared`, `run_event_map_closure`, `run_event_filter_closure`, `execute_block_call`'s own body loop) driving instructions through `VM::execute()` directly \u2014 whose switch has no case for the meta-opcodes, so any `BLOCK_CALL` inside a subprogram body silently no-op'd. A new `VM::execute_step(slot, body, ip) -> next_ip` helper owns the meta-opcode dispatch and is now the single entry point for both the main loop and every body runner; nested `BLOCK_CALL` (fn-in-fn, fn-in-event-map-closure, fn-in-voice-body) now dispatches correctly."

  "content": "Hypothesis: `op_filter_formant` was dominated by executing the six Chamberlin SVF steps (3 formant bands \u00d7 L/R) as scalar serial code every sample. I made one focused AVX2 change: a kernel that packs the six independent SVF updates into a single 8-lane pass per sample, while keeping the scalar parameter/caching logic and `std::tan` exact and retaining the original scalar body as Oracle/fallback.\n\nCheck result: build passed, fixed-stimulus equality passed as exact, and the short benchmark measured `4453.63 ns/block` vs `5835.97 ns/block` \u2014 about **1.31\u00d7 faster**.\n\nIDEA: serial 3-band \u00d7 L/R Chamberlin SVF inner loop -> 8-lane AVX2 bank pass over 3 formant bands \u00d7 2 channels (check: exact equality, 1.31x)"
